# Sounds and particles

Mechanics are identified from the 26.3 server source. Registry and resource entries are mechanically enumerated from the official reports, bootstrapped registries and built-in datapack.

| ID | Feature | Vanilla source | Test | Vanilla | Ferraloom |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| sounds-particles.sound.seed-range | Broadcast seeded sound events with source, pitch, volume and range to eligible players | `ServerLevel#playSeededSound; PlayerList#broadcast` | | - | todo |
| sounds-particles.sound.stop | Stop sounds by source and optional sound id for selected players | `StopSoundCommand; ClientboundStopSoundPacket` | | - | todo |
| sounds-particles.particles.parameters | Decode particle-type-specific parameters and validate command input | `ParticleTypes; ParticleOptions; ParticleArgument` | | - | todo |
| sounds-particles.particles.range | Select particle recipients by distance and forced-send flag | `ServerLevel#sendParticles` | | - | todo |
| sounds-particles.level-events | Broadcast numbered world events and global events with source-player exclusion | `ServerLevel#levelEvent; ServerLevel#globalLevelEvent` | | - | todo |
